# Block 133 — Keeley Electronics

## Scope
Builder → pedals only. Target categories are Overdrive, Distortion, and Fuzz. Non-dirt effects, standalone boosts, compression, modulation, delay/reverb, pitch effects, and utility functions are excluded unless a pedal explicitly contains a qualifying dirt section.

## Primary sources
- Keeley Electronics home: https://robertkeeley.com/
- Distortion / Drive / Boost / Fuzz category: https://robertkeeley.com/product-category/distortion-drive-boost-fuzz/
- Oxblood Overdrive: https://robertkeeley.com/product/keeley-oxblood-overdrive/
- Mk3 Driver: https://robertkeeley.com/product/mk3-driver-andy-timmons-full-range-overdrive/
- Super Rodent: https://robertkeeley.com/product/super-rodent-overdrive-and-distortion/
- Super Phat Mod: https://robertkeeley.com/product/super-phat-mod/
- Manis Overdrive: https://robertkeeley.com/product/manis-overdrive/
- Aria Compressor Drive: https://robertkeeley.com/product/aria-compressor-overdrive/
- Fuzz Bender: https://robertkeeley.com/product/fuzz-bender/
- Dark Side: https://robertkeeley.com/product/dark-side/
- Loomer: https://robertkeeley.com/product/loomer/
- SUNS Ultravoid Fuzz: https://robertkeeley.com/product/suns-ultravoid-fuzz/
- OCTA PSI: https://robertkeeley.com/product/octa-psi-transfigurating-fuzz/
- SUPER BASS: https://robertkeeley.com/product/super-bass-overdrive-and-distortion/

## Overdrive
- Oxblood Overdrive
- Mk3 Driver – Andy Timmons Full Range Overdrive
- Manis Overdrive
- Super Phat Mod
- Red Dirt Overdrive / Red Dirt Mini lineage
- D&M Drive
- Aria Compressor Drive
- Super Bass Overdrive and Distortion
- Tube Drive / Tube Drive Twin Triode lineage
- El Rey Dorado
- Noble Screamer

## Distortion
- Super Rodent Overdrive and Distortion
- Oxblood Overdrive
- Mk3 Driver – Andy Timmons Full Range Overdrive
- SUPER BASS Overdrive and Distortion
- Dark Side (fuzz/distortion section)
- OCTA PSI Transfigurating Fuzz (fuzz/distortion section)
- KO-ALS Distortion
- Filaments High-Gain Distortion
- Rotten Apple
- D&M Drive

## Fuzz
- Fuzz Bender
- Dark Side
- Loomer
- SUNS Ultravoid Fuzz
- OCTA PSI Transfigurating Fuzz
- Moon Fuzz / SUNS lineage
- Germanium Super Phat Mod
- Monterey Pop Fuzz
- Fuzz Head
- Psi Fuzz
- Loomer Fuzz/Reverb lineage

## Explicit overlaps
- **Oxblood Overdrive** — Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz capability. Keeley explicitly describes clean, distortion, and fuzz range, so the dirt census keeps it in all three target categories.
- **Mk3 Driver** — Overdrive + Fuzz. Keeley describes the pedal as an overdrive workstation ranging from clean boost through overdrive to square-wave/germanium fuzz. Distortion is present in the technical description, but the product identity is primarily full-range overdrive with a fuzz mode; keep it in Overdrive + Fuzz rather than multiplying it solely because the word distortion appears in the description.
- **Super Rodent** — Overdrive + Distortion. Officially presented as a 4-in-1 overdrive/distortion pedal combining two drive families and hybrid modes.
- **SUPER BASS** — Overdrive + Distortion. Official product page explicitly identifies both pedal types.
- **D&M Drive** — Overdrive + Distortion. Treat as one dual-drive lineage rather than separate channel products.
- **Dark Side** — Fuzz + Distortion. The fuzz section is the qualifying dirt component; delay/modulation are excluded.
- **Loomer** — Fuzz. Reverb is excluded. Despite the product being a multi-effect, its qualifying dirt identity is the wall-of-fuzz section.
- **OCTA PSI** — Fuzz + Distortion. The official page explicitly lists analog transistor fuzz and distortion alongside octave/pitch functions; pitch functions are excluded.
- **Germanium Super Phat Mod** — Overdrive + Fuzz. Keeley explicitly describes it as a combination of JFET overdrive and germanium fuzz.
- **Aria Compressor Drive** — Overdrive only. Compression is excluded even though it is an integral second half of the pedal.

## Historical / variant restraint
- Treat product revisions and normal enclosure/color changes as one pedal lineage.
- Do not create separate census entries for Custom Shop artwork, anniversary finishes, artist editions, Sweetwater colorways, or one-off cosmetic editions when the underlying pedal is the same.
- Keep Loomer as one fuzz lineage despite anniversary and artist editions.
- Keep OCTA PSI variants as one lineage unless a future source demonstrates a genuinely distinct dirt product identity rather than a modification/finish.
- Keep Oxblood and Oxblood Germanium within the same builder lineage, while noting the Germanium version only where its distinct fuzz behavior is explicitly documented.
- Do not count compressor, boost, modulation, delay/reverb, pitch/octave, or switching features as separate target categories.
- Do not infer a category from gain range alone. Use explicit builder/product descriptions.

## Verification notes
Keeley’s official dirt category currently includes products such as Tube Drive Twin Triode, Red Nectar Compressor and Overdrive, Manis Overdrive, SUPER BASS Overdrive and Distortion, and SUNS Ultravoid Fuzz. Individual official product pages were used to resolve ambiguous multi-effect and multi-mode pedals. The official descriptions for Oxblood, Mk3 Driver, Super Rodent, Fuzz Bender, Dark Side, Loomer, SUNS, OCTA PSI, and SUPER BASS provide direct evidence for the category assignments above.

# Block 134 — Black Arts Toneworks

## Scope
Builder → pedals only. Target categories are Overdrive, Distortion, and Fuzz. Non-dirt functions are excluded from the census. Cosmetic editions, custom-shop one-offs, and ordinary revisions are not counted as separate pedal identities unless they represent a distinct product.

## Primary source
- Black Arts Toneworks official pedal archive: https://www.blackartstoneworks.com/pedal/
- Black Arts Toneworks official site: https://www.blackartstoneworks.com/

## Overdrive
- BAT — official description says it can act as a boost or driver and become a standalone distortion.
- Raw Heart OD — distortion/overdrive design, explicitly built around distortion use.
- Black Forest — official archive describes it as a dirt/overdrive design with added fuzz range.
- Tres Diablos Ruidosos — ranges from boost and edgy OD through distortion and into oversaturated fuzz.
- Witch Burner MkII — overdrive.
- Pharaoh — primarily fuzz, but official description explicitly covers light OD; counted as Fuzz + Overdrive.
- Revelation — amp-pusher/drive; distortion gain section is central to its operation, counted as Overdrive + Distortion.

## Distortion
- BAT — standalone distortion capability explicitly stated by builder.
- Raw Heart OD — builder describes it as a distortion pedal; despite the OD name, counted as Overdrive + Distortion.
- Quantum Mystic — distortion.
- Priestess — fuzz/distortion design; counted as Fuzz + Distortion.
- Black Forest — overdrive/fuzz design with higher-gain dirt; counted as Overdrive + Fuzz rather than inventing a separate distortion identity.
- Tres Diablos Ruidosos — explicitly reaches classic 70s rock distortion and proto-metal crunch; counted as Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz.
- Revelation — explicit distortion gain function; counted as Overdrive + Distortion.
- FNORD — official description combines octaver/fuzz with traditional fuzz/distortion; counted as Fuzz + Distortion.
- Destroyer — combined Ritual/Oath design described as fuzz and amp-slammer; counted as Fuzz + Distortion only where the builder's description supports the distortion/preamp role.

## Fuzz
- Pharaoh — classic fuzz roots and saturated fuzz range.
- LSTR — official archive describes it as delivering more fuzz than its Pharaoh relative.
- Black Sheep — Fuzz.
- Black Forest — Overdrive/Fuzz.
- Priestess — Fuzz + Distortion.
- FNORD — Octaver/Fuzz with traditional fuzz/distortion.
- Rabid Mammal — fuzz section based on a modified Supreme/Pharaoh-family design.
- Destroyer — controllable wall of fuzz combined with a second amp-slamming section.
- Sarcophagus — dual Pharaoh/LSTR; counted as Fuzz, with the order-switch function excluded.
- Coven — dual Pharaoh/Black Forest; counted as Fuzz + Overdrive because those are the underlying dirt identities.
- Son of Pharaoh — Pharaoh-family fuzz.
- Pharaoh Supreme — Pharaoh-family fuzz.
- Witch Burner MkII — not counted as fuzz merely because it can become fuzzy; primary identity is overdrive.

## Explicit overlaps
- Pharaoh = Overdrive + Fuzz
- Raw Heart OD = Overdrive + Distortion
- Revelation = Overdrive + Distortion
- Black Forest = Overdrive + Fuzz
- Tres Diablos Ruidosos = Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz
- Priestess = Distortion + Fuzz
- FNORD = Distortion + Fuzz
- Destroyer = Distortion + Fuzz
- Coven = Overdrive + Fuzz
- Sarcophagus = Fuzz; its two underlying circuits do not create a separate new category beyond the dirt identities already represented.

## Historical / variant restraint
- Pharaoh revisions and cosmetic/Blackout editions are consolidated into the Pharaoh lineage.
- Rabid Mammal V2 and earlier Rabid Mammal/custom-shop form are one lineage.
- Witch Burner revisions are consolidated unless a clearly distinct product is documented.
- Custom Shop one-offs, B-stock, special enclosure colors, and artwork variants are excluded.
- Boost, octave, preamp, and switching functions are not counted as separate target categories unless the builder explicitly describes the same pedal as an Overdrive, Distortion, or Fuzz device.
- No components, schematics, PCB information, BOMs, gutshots, cloning instructions, or construction details are included.

## Verification note
The official Black Arts Toneworks archive currently lists BAT, Black Forest, Black Sheep, Coven, Crown of Horns, Destroyer, FNORD, LSTR, Pharaoh, Pharaoh Supreme, Priestess, Quantum Mystic, Rabid Mammal, Raw Heart OD, Revelation, Sarcophagus, Skyboost, Son of Pharaoh, Tres Diablos Ruidosos, and Witch Burner MkII. Individual official pages were used where needed to resolve category overlap and historical status.

## Next
Block 135.

# Block 135 — Death By Audio

## Scope
Builder → Pedals only. Target categories are Overdrive, Distortion, and Fuzz. Non-target effects and secondary functions are excluded. Ordinary revisions, editions, and cosmetic variants are consolidated unless the builder presents them as distinct dirt products.

## Builder
**Death By Audio**

## Primary source basis
- https://deathbyaudio.com/collections/overdrive
- https://deathbyaudio.com/collections/distortion
- https://deathbyaudio.com/collections/fuzz
- https://deathbyaudio.com/collections/all-pedals
- Individual official product pages for products listed below.

## Overdrive
- Interstellar Overdriver
- Interstellar Overdriver Deluxe
- Thee Treble Overload
- Fuzz War (overdrive range explicitly described, but retained primarily as Fuzz; see overlap notes)
- Germanium Filter (overdrive range explicitly described, but retained primarily as Distortion/Fuzz; see overlap notes)

## Distortion
- Germanium Filter
- Absolute Destruction
- Fuzz War
- Apocalypse
- Bass War
- Thee Fuzz War Overload / Fuzz Warr Overload lineage
- Sonic Incinerator
- Harmonic Transformer

## Fuzz
- Fuzz War
- Apocalypse
- Bass War
- Crossover Fuzz
- Germanium Filter
- Absolute Destruction
- Fuzz Gun
- Supersonic Fuzz Gun
- Octave Clang
- Reverberation Machine (fuzz content not treated as a standalone category; reverb excluded)

## Explicit overlap handling
- **Fuzz War**: Fuzz + Distortion + Overdrive. The official product description explicitly describes its sweep from boost/overdrive through thick distortion to full fuzz.
- **Germanium Filter**: Distortion + Fuzz + Overdrive range. Official description says the gain moves from dirty boost through saturated drive and distortion to fuzz.
- **Apocalypse**: Fuzz + Distortion + Overdrive range. The official description presents multiple post-amplifier paths and explicitly describes distortion/fuzz plus cleaner overdrive/boost settings.
- **Bass War**: Fuzz + Distortion + Overdrive range. Official description explicitly describes drive, distortion, and fuzz settings.
- **Absolute Destruction**: Distortion + Fuzz. Its official description explicitly covers crushing distortion, thick fuzz, and octave-like destructive behavior. Octave behavior is not separately counted.
- **Thee Treble Overload**: Overdrive/Distortion treatment is retained as Overdrive because the official product is presented as a treble-overdrive/overload device. Its high-gain distortion capability does not create a separate distortion product entry.
- **Crossover Fuzz**: Fuzz only. Its filter/crossover/stereo/CV functions are excluded from the dirt census.

## Exclusions / restraint
- Delay, reverb, modulation, filter, vocal, and experimental pedals without a clear standalone OD/distortion/fuzz identity are excluded.
- Secondary gain behavior alone does not create an additional category unless the official builder description clearly presents the product as functioning in that dirt family.
- Do not multiply cosmetic editions or ordinary revisions into separate builder relationships.
- No photos, biographies, component inventories, schematics, PCB/BOM information, gutshots, cloning information, or later archive phases.

## Verification note
Death By Audio's current official site explicitly separates its pedal catalog into Overdrive, Distortion, and Fuzz collections, and individual product descriptions provide the basis for overlap decisions. The Fuzz War, Germanium Filter, Apocalypse, Bass War, and Absolute Destruction pages were especially useful for distinguishing primary dirt identity from secondary gain behavior.

## Overdrive
- Apollo VII / Apollo VIIb
- Polaris
- Aphelion

- Rumblefuzz / Rumblefuzz II

## Explicit overlaps
- Titan II = Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz. Official description explicitly describes low-gain overdrive, fuzz-tinged distortion, and full fuzz.
- Rumblefuzz II = Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz. Official description explicitly states overdrive, distortion, and fuzz levels of gain.
- Charon = Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz. Official description moves from low-gain crunchy overdrive through saturated distortion to fuzz.
- Saturn V = Overdrive. Although primarily a booster, the builder explicitly describes it as low-gain overdrive when the Drive control is advanced.
- Nebula = Fuzz. Octave and clean blend functions are not counted as separate dirt categories.

## Variant / lineage restraint
- Sputnik family editions and enclosure/reissue changes are consolidated as one lineage where the underlying product identity is the same.
- Apollo VII / VIIb are treated as one product lineage; Cadet and PDX/custom availability are not multiplied into separate pedal identities.
- Charon original and Charon v2 are treated as one Charon lineage, while the v2 Mod is retained as a documented evolution rather than a new unrelated builder entry.
- Rumblefuzz / Rumblefuzz II are treated as one lineage with II documented as the later version.
- Gemini III / IV remain identifiable generations of the Dual Fuzz Generator lineage rather than cosmetic duplicates.
- Limited colors, signed/numbered editions, NOS listings, and enclosure treatments do not create additional builder-pedal relationships.
- Boost, preamp, tremolo, phaser, compressor, filter, EQ, reverb, and other non-target functions are excluded unless the pedal explicitly qualifies for Overdrive, Distortion, or Fuzz.

## Research restraint
No photos, biographies, components, schematics, PCB work, BOMs, internal-build information, cloning information, or later pedal-info fields were added.

## Next
Block 137 — previously uncovered builder; verify against primary/authoritative sources before saving.

# Block 137 — ThorpyFX

## Scope
Builder → Pedals only. Target categories: Overdrive, Distortion, Fuzz. Non-target functions are not counted as separate dirt categories.

## Sources
- ThorpyFX official pedal catalog: https://thorpyfx.com/en-us/collections/thorpyfx-pedals
- ThorpyFX official overdrive catalog: https://thorpyfx.com/en-us/collections/overdrive
- ThorpyFX official fuzz catalog: https://thorpyfx.com/collections/overdrive-pedals-copy
- ThorpyFX official all-products catalog: https://thorpyfx.com/en-us/collections/all

## Overdrive
- Gunshot
- Peacekeeper
- The Dane
- The Dane MKI
- The KUDU
- Bunker
- Electric Lightning
- The J
- Scarlet Tunic
- The Old Timer

## Distortion
- Warthog
- Scarlet Tunic
- The Old Timer

## Fuzz
- Fallout Cloud
- Field Marshal
- WOPR
- Six Zero
- Shaftesbury Duo Fuzz
- The Old Timer

## Explicit overlaps
- Scarlet Tunic = Overdrive + Distortion. Its primary identity is amp-emulation/drive, so no additional non-target category is created.
- The Old Timer = Overdrive + Distortion + Fuzz. It is a three-section dirt pedal, so all three target categories are retained.
- WOPR = Fuzz. Boost is excluded as a non-target function.
- Six Zero = Fuzz. Tremolo is excluded as a non-target function.

## Variant / historical restraint
- MKI/MKII and special-edition treatments are consolidated where they represent the same pedal lineage rather than separate builder relationships.
- Birthday/heritage/artwork editions are not counted as new pedals.
- Bundles are excluded.
- Boost, compressor, modulation, and other non-target functions are excluded from the dirt census.
- No components, schematics, PCB, BOM, gutshots, cloning, or build instructions are included.

## Verification note
The official ThorpyFX catalog currently identifies pedals such as KUDU, Bunker, Electric Lightning, Peacekeeper and Gunshot as overdrive products; Warthog as distortion; and Fallout Cloud, Field Marshal and WOPR as fuzz products. The catalog also identifies Scarlet Tunic and The Old Timer as multi-function drive products. These relationships were used as the basis for this census block.

# Block 138 — ProCo Sound

## Scope
Builder → Pedals only. Target categories: Overdrive, Distortion, Fuzz. Non-target functions are not counted as separate dirt categories.

## Sources
- ProCo / RAT official product information and RAT catalog material: https://www.procosound.com/
- RAT Distortion product catalog: https://www.procosound.com/
- Current RAT product listings and product descriptions were cross-checked against current dealer catalog evidence where the original ProCo catalog was difficult to index.

## Overdrive
- SOLO Analog Distortion and Overdrive
- Juggernaut / Bass RAT

## Distortion
- RAT
- RAT2
- Lil' RAT
- Turbo RAT
- You Dirty RAT
- FAT RAT
- Deucetone RAT
- SOLO Analog Distortion and Overdrive
- Juggernaut / Bass RAT
- BRAT / Roadkill
- Vintage RAT Reissue
- '85 Whiteface RAT Reissue
- R2DU

## Fuzz
- None identified as a distinct ProCo target-category product in the verified catalog material.

## Explicit overlaps
- SOLO = Overdrive + Distortion. ProCo describes it as an analog distortion and overdrive pedal, so both target categories are retained.
- Juggernaut / Bass RAT = Overdrive + Distortion. Its documented controls and description explicitly include adjustable overdrive behavior while its core identity is a bass distortion unit.
- RAT-family products remain Distortion even when their gain range can produce fuzz-like sounds. Fuzz is not added merely because a distortion pedal can become very saturated.

## RAT lineage / variant restraint
- The original RAT, RAT2, Turbo RAT, You Dirty RAT, FAT RAT, Lil' RAT and Deucetone RAT remain distinct named products because ProCo presented them as separate products with materially different clipping approaches, formats, or channel arrangements.
- Cosmetic revisions, enclosure revisions, anniversary colors, dealer exclusives, and other appearance-only treatments are not multiplied into separate builder relationships.
- The original Bud Box RAT and successive early enclosure versions are treated as the RAT lineage rather than separate modern product relationships unless a separately named product identity exists.
- R2DU is retained as a distinct named product because it was a rack-format dual RAT product rather than merely a cosmetic revision.
- BRAT / Roadkill is treated as one named product lineage rather than two separate pedals.
- Vintage RAT and '85 Whiteface reissues are retained as historically distinct named reissue products, while cosmetic editions of those products are not multiplied.
- Juggernaut is retained because it was a separately named bass-focused product, not merely a RAT enclosure revision.

## Category restraint
- Distortion remains the dominant RAT-family category. A RAT's ability to operate at lower gain or become extremely saturated does not automatically create an Overdrive or Fuzz relationship.
- Boost, effects-loop, clean-blend, filtering, and other utility functions are not counted as additional target categories.
- No components, schematics, PCB, BOM, gutshots, cloning, or build instructions are included.

## Verification note
Current product listings identify RAT2, Lil' RAT, Turbo RAT, You Dirty RAT, FAT RAT and Deucetone RAT as RAT distortion products, while SOLO is explicitly described as an analog distortion and overdrive pedal. Historical catalog evidence also identifies the Juggernaut as a bass distortion product with adjustable overdrive behavior. The census therefore keeps the RAT family primarily under Distortion, with explicit Overdrive overlap only where the product description supports it.

## Next
Block 139.

# Block 139 — Xotic Effects

## Scope
Builder → Pedals only. Target categories: Overdrive, Distortion, Fuzz. Non-target functions are not counted as separate dirt categories.

## Sources
- Xotic official effects catalog: https://xotic.us/effects/
- Xotic official manuals: https://xotic.us/manuals/
- Xotic official shop: https://users-new.xotic.us/shop/category/xotic-pedals-1
- Xotic official About/updates: https://xotic.us/about-us/

## Overdrive
- AC Booster
- AC Booster V2
- AC Booster Comp
- AC Plus
- BB Preamp
- BB Preamp V1.5
- BB Preamp Comp
- BB Preamp MB
- BB Preamp AT
- BB Plus
- Soul Driven
- Soul Driven AH
- Bass BB Preamp
- Bass BB Preamp V1.5
- SL Drive

## Distortion
- SL Drive

## Fuzz
- No verified Xotic standalone fuzz product found in the official catalog reviewed for this block.

## Explicit overlaps
- SL Drive = Overdrive + Distortion. Xotic's current catalog explicitly labels it "DISTORTION / OVERDRIVE."
- BB Preamp family = Overdrive. The official catalog describes the current BB Preamp V1.5 as boost/overdrive, while legacy BB variants remain part of the same overdrive lineage.
- AC Booster family = Overdrive. The current AC Booster V2 is described by Xotic as expressive overdrive; legacy AC Booster variants remain in the same drive family.
- Soul Driven = Overdrive. The official manual explicitly includes a "Full Harmonic Overdrive" setting.
- Bass BB Preamp products = Overdrive. Xotic explicitly describes them as bass boost/overdrive products.

## Variant / historical restraint
- Current revisions and artist editions are consolidated by pedal lineage where they represent the same underlying builder relationship.
- Custom Shop variants such as BB Preamp MB, BB Preamp Comp, AC Booster Comp, and artist versions are retained as named historical relationships but are not multiplied into unrelated products.
- Boost-only products such as EP Booster, RC Booster, AH Booster, Super Sweet Booster, and Super Clean Buffer are excluded because boost is outside the target categories.
- Compression, wah, volume, filter, signal-blender, and power accessories are excluded.
- No components, schematics, PCB, BOM, gutshots, cloning, or build instructions are included.

## Verification note
Xotic's official catalog currently lists SL Drive as a "DISTORTION / OVERDRIVE" product, BB Preamp V1.5 as boost/overdrive, and Soul Driven as a mid-boost overdrive. Xotic's official manuals also describe Soul Driven as capable of full harmonic overdrive. These relationships form the basis of this census block. No official Xotic fuzz product was verified in the reviewed catalog.

# Block 140 — Way Huge

## Scope
Builder → Pedals only. Target categories: Overdrive, Distortion, Fuzz. Non-target functions are not counted as separate dirt categories.

## Sources
- Jim Dunlop official Way Huge catalog: https://www.jimdunlop.com/way-huge/
- Jim Dunlop official Way Huge current catalog: https://www.jimdunlop.com/products/electronics/way-huge/
- Jim Dunlop official Way Huge legacy catalog: https://www.jimdunlop.com/products/electronics/legacy-products/way-huge/
- Jim Dunlop official Way Huge product/manual pages for individual pedals.
- Jim Dunlop official Fat Sandwich demo: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0hSb3ncRXNo

## Overdrive
- Red Llama
- Green Rhino
- Conspiracy Theory
- STO
- Supa-Lead
- Overrated Special
- Geisha Drive
- Way Huge Overdrive / Saucy Box Overdrive lineage
- Penny Saver Royale
- Camel Toe Triple Overdrive
- Pork Loin

## Distortion
- Fat Sandwich Harmonic Saturator
- Geisha Drive
- Conquistador Fuzzstortion

## Fuzz
- Swollen Pickle
- Russian Pickle
- Stone Burner Sub Atomic Fuzz
- Havalina Germanium Fuzz
- Conquistador Fuzzstortion
- Pork & Pickle
- Doom Hammer Fuzz

## Explicit overlaps
- Geisha Drive = Overdrive + Distortion. Jim Dunlop describes it as moving from overdrive into distortion territory across its Drive range.
- Conquistador Fuzzstortion = Fuzz + Distortion. The official product page identifies it as an aggressive gated fuzz and names it Fuzzstortion.
- Pork & Pickle = Overdrive + Fuzz. The official product page explicitly identifies it as Overdrive & Fuzz and combines the Pork Loin overdrive with Russian-Pickle fuzz.
- Penny Saver Royale = Overdrive plus modulation. Only the overdrive function is counted here; the modulation side is excluded.
- Stone Burner = Fuzz plus sub-octave. Only Fuzz is counted.
- Camel Toe = Overdrive. Its separate Green Rhino and Red Llama sections remain part of the combined triple-overdrive product relationship rather than creating additional category entries.

## Variant / historical restraint
- Swollen Pickle XXX and Swollen Pickle MKIIS are consolidated into the Swollen Pickle lineage rather than treated as separate builder relationships.
- Green Rhino Mk IV/Mk V and other edition changes are consolidated into the Green Rhino lineage.
- Russian-Pickle original and Smalls versions are consolidated into one lineage.
- Geisha Drive and Geisha Drive Blue are consolidated into one lineage; finish/artwork changes do not create another pedal relationship.
- Conspiracy Theory and the Deep State Conspiracy Theory edition are consolidated.
- Red Llama compact/reissue treatment is consolidated with the Red Llama lineage.
- Product parts listings are used only as corroboration that the pedal existed; parts themselves are not archive entries.
- Attack Vector, Aqua-Puss, Blue Hippo, Echo-Puss, Supa-Puss, Saffron Squeeze, Atreides, and other non-target effects are excluded from the dirt census.
- No components, schematics, PCB, BOM, gutshots, cloning, or build instructions are included.

## Verification note
Jim Dunlop's official Way Huge catalog currently lists Swollen Pickle variants, Doom Hammer Fuzz, Pork & Pickle Overdrive & Fuzz, Conspiracy Theory Professional Overdrive, and Green Rhino Overdrive. Its official legacy catalog also lists Stone Burner, STO, Green Rhino Mk V, Geisha Drive, Red Llama, Penny Saver Royale, Russian Pickle, Supa-Lead, Overrated Special, Conquistador Fuzzstortion, and Camel Toe Triple Overdrive. Official product documentation also explicitly describes Fat Sandwich as a distortion product, Geisha Drive as spanning overdrive to distortion territory, Stone Burner as fuzz, and Pork & Pickle as overdrive plus fuzz.

# Block 141 — ZVEX Effects

## Scope
Builder → Pedals only. Target categories: Overdrive, Distortion, Fuzz. Non-target functions are not counted as separate dirt categories.

## Sources
- ZVEX official guitar-pedal catalog: https://www.zvex.com/guitar-pedals
- ZVEX official homepage/current catalog: https://www.zvex.com/
- ZVEX official 2019 catalog PDF: https://www.zvex.com/s/12-pg-catalog-2019.pdf
- ZVEX official product pages for Fuzz Factory, Box of Rock, Distortron, '59 Sound, Basstortion and related pedals.

## Overdrive
- Channel 2

## Distortion
- Box of Metal
- Box of Rock
- Distortron
- Double Rock
- '59 Sound
- Basstortion

## Fuzz
- Fuzz Factory
- Fat Fuzz Factory
- Fuzz Factory 7
- Woolly Mammoth
- Woolly Mammoth 7
- Fuzzolo
- Mastotron
- Octane 3
- Machine
- Fuzz Probe

## Explicit overlaps
- Channel 2 = Overdrive. ZVEX's official 2019 catalog explicitly describes it as ranging from a unity-gain line buffer to a light transparent overdrive.
- Box of Rock = Distortion. ZVEX's current catalog places it in Distortion; its built-in high-headroom boost is excluded as a non-target function.
- Double Rock = Distortion. The official product page describes configurations including two Distortrons and a Box of Rock, while boost functions remain outside the census.
- '59 Sound = Distortion. ZVEX's official page describes it as a distortion pedal modeled on a 1959 Tweed Bassman.
- Basstortion = Distortion. The official page describes it as capturing tube-amp distortion for bass.
- Fuzz Factory family = Fuzz. Fuzz Factory 7, Vexter, Vertical and artwork editions are treated as the same core fuzz lineage where they are presentation or edition variants.
- Woolly Mammoth family = Fuzz. Woolly Mammoth 7 and Vexter versions remain within the same fuzz lineage.
- Machine = Fuzz. ZVEX explicitly calls it a unique fuzz; its unusual oscillator behavior does not create a separate target category.

## Variant / historical restraint
- Vexter, Vertical, hand-painted, custom-art, anniversary, engraved, and similar presentation variants are not multiplied into separate builder relationships when the underlying pedal remains the same lineage.
- Fuzz Factory 7 is retained as a named family generation, but its Vexter/custom-art presentations are consolidated.
- Woolly Mammoth 7 is retained with the Woolly Mammoth family rather than multiplying every artwork or Vexter version.
- Custom one-offs and Mod Bench listings are excluded unless they represent an established standard pedal relationship.
- The Sylvia Massy Woolly Mammoth special release remains within the Woolly Mammoth fuzz lineage.
- Super Hard On and Super Duper products are excluded because their official identity is boost; Channel 2 is included because ZVEX explicitly describes it as light overdrive in the 2019 catalog.
- Modulation, octave, sampler, tremolo, phaser, filter, meter and other non-target effects are excluded.
- No components, schematics, PCB, BOM, gutshots, cloning, or build instructions are included.

## Verification note
ZVEX's current official guitar-pedal catalog separates its products into categories including Distortion and Fuzz. The current catalog lists Box of Rock, Distortron, Double Rock, '59 Sound and Basstortion under Distortion, while listing Fuzz Factory, Fat Fuzz Factory, Woolly Mammoth, Fuzzolo, Mastotron, Octane 3, Machine and Fuzz Probe under Fuzz. ZVEX's 2019 official catalog explicitly describes Channel 2 as capable of light transparent overdrive and describes Box of Metal as a distortion pedal. These relationships form the basis of this census block.
